Milei's actions have led to Argentina regaining a favourable relation with the IMF after having been in a precarious position for almost 2 years, securing a 20billion dollar loan. Since July 2022, the country has consistently kept a risk assessment of over 2000 points, well above the continental average of 250 points, marking significant volatility and lack of trust in the market. Since Milei's victory, the risk assessment trended downward, falling below 500 points for the first time since 2018. The loan is set to be used to disintegrate the remaining capital controls on the peso, as well as aiding the government in building up reserves, which had been exhausted prior to his presidency. [J. P. Morgan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/JPMorgan_Chase "JPMorgan Chase") has commented that "The policy advancements represent a significant step forward, enabling the country to unlock a potential that has been stifled for decades due to poor policy-making." Argentina is the IMF's largest debtor country.[\[198\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Argentina's_risk_index_rebounds_aft-200)[\[250\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-252)[\[251\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-253)[\[252\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-254)[\[197\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Argentina_investors_bet_on_Milei's-199) The possibility of a peronist victory led towards the market reacting with fear, resulting in the peso depreciating and the central bank having to spend a billion dollars to maintain the currency in its bandwidth. The market risk index surged due to this reaction.[\[209\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-auto13-211)[\[208\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-auto11-210)

By June 2025 foreign inflows of cash grew constrained in Argentina falling 54% in 2024, and as of April 2025 stands at a $3billion deficit.[\[253\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-255)[\[254\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-256)

In September 2025, the possibility of Milei losing the midterm elections sent shockwaves throughout the Argentine market, leading towards intervention by the central bank.[\[255\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-257)[\[208\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-auto11-210) U.S. Secretary Scott Bessent's announcement that a 20 billion dollar swap line for Argentina was ready halted the market meltdown, leaving it in a fragile state.[\[256\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-258) Milei's attempt on stabilizing the currency has led towards an increase in purchasing power, which Argentines have exploited by travelling abroad. This has cost the government billions.[\[257\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-259)

Shortly after Milei's strong performance and victory in the 2025 legislative elections, the Argentinian bond market surged, with dollar notes due in 2035 having increased by 23% in one day.[\[212\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-auto14-214) Argentina's risk index plummeted by 40% (1081 to 652) in the same day.[\[213\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-auto15-215)[\[258\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-260)

Seeing the relative stability of Argentina's economy post elections, US banks revisited the deal for a 20 billion dollar swap, downsizing it to 5 billion dollars.[\[259\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-261)

#### GDP

In the first and second quarter of 2024, Argentina's GDP contracted by 2.1% and 1.8%. However, in the last quarter, it expanded 3.9%, despite the significant austerity implemented by the President. Conversely, agriculture rebounded dramatically from a severe drought, achieving an impressive growth rate of 80.2% as conditions improved. The economy of Argentina is projected by the BBVA to grow by 5.5% in 2025, and by Goldman Sachs by 3.5% and 4.5% by the Argentine central bank, as inflation stabilizes and investment starts pouring in, due to Milei's liberalization of the economy. The financial improvements seen in the 3rd quarter of 2024 are complemented by society's renewed confidence in the government, creating a positive outlook for 2025.[\[191\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-auto2-193)[\[192\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-GDP_rebounds_in_the_third_quarter-194)[\[193\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-auto4-195)[\[194\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-americaeconomia.com-196)[\[195\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-auto-197)[\[196\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-ReferenceB-198) Economic activity during January 2025 returned to levels not seen since July 2022, trended to increase. The volume of real loans towards the private sector is at an all-time high in both dollar and peso denominations.[\[187\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-milei.ufm.edu-189) GDP has proven difficult to increase under austerity, with economists revisioning their 2025 forecast for growth to 5% as the economy shrank by 0.1% in May of the same year.[\[260\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-262)

[Moody's](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moody's_Ratings "Moody's Ratings") have increased Argentina's rating from 'Ca' to 'Caa3' due to the reforms initiated by President Milei.[\[261\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-263)

According to CNN, Argentina's stock index MERVAL soared by 22% one day after Milei's victory in the legislative elections of 2025, indicating significant trust in the Argentine government's ability to continue its liberalizing reforms.[\[262\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-264)

### Foreign policy

See also: [List of international presidential trips made by Javier Milei](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_international_presidential_trips_made_by_Javier_Milei "List of international presidential trips made by Javier Milei")

[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c2/President_Donald_Trump_meets_with_Argentina%E2%80%99s_President_Javier_Milei_%2854348219476%29.jpg/250px-President_Donald_Trump_meets_with_Argentina%E2%80%99s_President_Javier_Milei_%2854348219476%29.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:President_Donald_Trump_meets_with_Argentina%E2%80%99s_President_Javier_Milei_(54348219476).jpg) Milei shaking hands with [Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Donald_Trump "Donald Trump") in February 2025[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0a/Prime_Minister_Narendra_Modi_meets_the_President_of_Argentina%2C_Mr._Javier_Gerardo_Milei_on_his_arrival_at_Presidential_Palace%2C_in_Buenos_Aires%2C_Argentina_on_July_05%2C_2025_%281%29.jpg/250px-thumbnail.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Prime_Minister_Narendra_Modi_meets_the_President_of_Argentina,_Mr._Javier_Gerardo_Milei_on_his_arrival_at_Presidential_Palace,_in_Buenos_Aires,_Argentina_on_July_05,_2025_(1).jpg) Milei with Indian prime minister [Narendra Modi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Narendra_Modi "Narendra Modi") in July 2025

Milei made a radical change to Argentina's traditional foreign policy,[\[263\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-265) aligning Argentina with the United States and Israel.[\[264\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-266)[\[265\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-267) Some analysts, like Ayelén Oliva of the [BBC News](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BBC_News "BBC News") or Walter Schmidt of [Clarín](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Clar%C3%ADn_(Argentine_newspaper) "Clarín (Argentine newspaper)"), have compared his rapprochement to these countries to [President Menem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carlos_Menem "Carlos Menem")'s similar policies in the 1990s.[\[266\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-268)[\[267\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-269)

Milei's views on the [Russian invasion of Ukraine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_invasion_of_Ukraine "Russian invasion of Ukraine") had distanced him from other right-wing figures.[\[268\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-270) He first supported Ukraine in its defense against Russia, and [sent military helicopters to Ukraine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_military_aid_to_Ukraine_during_the_Russo-Ukrainian_War "List of military aid to Ukraine during the Russo-Ukrainian War") during the first months of his presidency.[\[269\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-271) Milei also cooled relations with Russia, and has considered sending more lethal aid to Ukraine.[\[270\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-272) However, since the change in U.S. administration in 2025, Milei's government started backing U.S.-led [peace efforts](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peace_negotiations_in_the_Russian_invasion_of_Ukraine "Peace negotiations in the Russian invasion of Ukraine").[\[271\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-273)

During the [Gaza war](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaza_war "Gaza war"), Milei was a fierce supporter of Israel, vowing his "unwavering" support.[\[272\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-274) Among his measures in support of Israel, Milei declared [Hamas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hamas "Hamas") as a terrorist organization in July 2024,[\[273\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-275) and ordered the Argentine delegation at the UN to [vote against the incorporation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_General_Assembly_Resolution_ES-10/23 "United Nations General Assembly Resolution ES-10/23") of the [State of Palestine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/State_of_Palestine "State of Palestine") into the United Nations.[\[274\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-276) In February 2024, Milei visited Israel and went to a [kibbutz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kibbutz "Kibbutz") that had been attacked by Hamas militants during the [October 7 attacks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/October_7_attacks "October 7 attacks").[\[275\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-277) After the confirmation of the deaths of [Bibas family](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kidnapping_and_killing_of_the_Bibas_family "Kidnapping and killing of the Bibas family") (who were Argentine citizens kidnapped by Hamas), Milei declared two days of national mourning upon the return of the family's bodies on 21 February 2025.[\[276\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-278)

In Latin America, Milei dismissed the results of the [2024 Venezuelan presidential election](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Venezuelan_presidential_election "2024 Venezuelan presidential election") as "fraudulent" and called on [Nicolás Maduro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nicol%C3%A1s_Maduro "Nicolás Maduro") to resign.[\[277\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-279) On 7 August 2024, Argentina became one of the first nations to recognize [Edmundo González](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edmundo_Gonz%C3%A1lez "Edmundo González") as the legitimate President of Venezuela.[\[278\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-280) Milei also met with U.S. Secretary of State [Marco Rubio](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marco_Rubio "Marco Rubio") during the [second inauguration of Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Second_inauguration_of_Donald_Trump "Second inauguration of Donald Trump") and reaffirmed the intention to continue to pressure Maduro to exit power.[\[279\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-281) As a result of the ensuing tensions, Venezuela ordered Milei's arrest and Argentina ordered Maduro's arrest.[\[280\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-282)

[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/be/Milei_condecoration_Israel_Apr_2026.jpg/250px-Milei_condecoration_Israel_Apr_2026.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Milei_condecoration_Israel_Apr_2026.jpg) Milei receives the [Israeli Presidential Medal of Honour](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_Presidential_Medal_of_Honour "Israeli Presidential Medal of Honour") from Israeli President [Isaac Herzog](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Isaac_Herzog "Isaac Herzog") during [Milei's visit to Israel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_state_visit_by_Javier_Milei_to_Israel "2026 state visit by Javier Milei to Israel") in April 2026

Milei has expressed discontent or strong ideological differences with China,[\[281\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-twnarg-283) even threatening to cut ties with China during his presidential campaign.[\[282\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-congratulationsfromtaiwan-284) In the immediate aftermath of Milei's electoral victory, the [government of Taiwan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Government_of_Taiwan "Government of Taiwan") congratulated Milei and said that Taiwan would seek to strengthen ties with Argentina.[\[282\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-congratulationsfromtaiwan-284) Moreover, it was rumored in December 2023, that then Foreign Minister [Diana Mondino](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diana_Mondino "Diana Mondino") had held a meeting with a Taiwanese official, raising suspicions of Taiwanese financing of Milei's presidential campaign.[\[283\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-meatandsoytrade-285) In response to these allegations, China's ambassador to Argentina Wang Wei issued a statement reaffirming the principle of a [One-China](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/One-China "One-China"), adding that Taiwan is an "inalienable part of China" and an internal affair as well.[\[284\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-lanacionargjan2024-286) Following threats by the Chinese government to stop buying soy and meat from Argentina in response to perceived ties between the two governments, the Argentine government clarified that there were no official contacts between Argentine and Taiwanese officials.[\[283\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-meatandsoytrade-285)[\[284\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-lanacionargjan2024-286) In January 2026, Milei said that his position on China has not changed, adding that trade agreements and other contacts with China respond to "pragmatic reasons" and not to ideological changes about the Chinese government.[\[285\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-china2026-287)

Milei is also a strong opponent of the [Communist Party of Cuba](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Communist_Party_of_Cuba "Communist Party of Cuba") and Cuban government, calling [First Secretary of the Communist Party of Cuba](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/First_Secretary_of_the_Communist_Party_of_Cuba "First Secretary of the Communist Party of Cuba") [Miguel Díaz-Canel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miguel_D%C3%ADaz-Canel "Miguel Díaz-Canel") "despicable" and referring to Cuba as a "prison island".[\[286\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-288) In October 2024, Milei fired foreign minister [Diana Mondino](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diana_Mondino "Diana Mondino") after she failed to vote for the [US embargo on Cuba](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_embargo_against_Cuba "United States embargo against Cuba") at the UN, later assuring that Argentina condemns the "Cuban dictatorship".[\[287\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-289) Milei has also condemned other leftist leaders in the region, calling Colombian President [Gustavo Petro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gustavo_Petro "Gustavo Petro") a "murderous terrorist"[\[288\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-290) and Brazilian President [Lula da Silva](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lula_da_Silva "Lula da Silva") "corrupt" and "communist".[\[289\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-291) In July 2024, Milei had a diplomatic confrontation with Bolivian President [Luis Arce](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Luis_Arce "Luis Arce") over the [2024 Bolivian coup attempt](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Bolivian_coup_attempt "2024 Bolivian coup attempt"), which Milei dismissed as "false" and orchestrated by Arce himself.[\[290\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-292)

[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/d/d9/Casa_Rosada_15_%2854033675711%29.jpg/250px-Casa_Rosada_15_%2854033675711%29.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Casa_Rosada_15_(54033675711).jpg) Javier Milei and Salvadoran President [Nayib Bukele](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nayib_Bukele "Nayib Bukele").

Milei enjoys a friendly personal relationship with former Brazilian president [Jair Bolsonaro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jair_Bolsonaro "Jair Bolsonaro") and U.S. president [Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Donald_Trump "Donald Trump"),[\[291\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-293)[\[292\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-294) and attended Trump's [second inauguration](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Second_inauguration_of_Donald_Trump "Second inauguration of Donald Trump") as president in January 2025 in Washington. Similar to Trump's first measures, Milei expressed intention to withdraw Argentina from the [Paris Accord](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paris_Agreement "Paris Agreement") and the [World Health Organization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Health_Organization "World Health Organization") (WHO), saying that the government is studying the possibilities of suspending Argentina's memberships.[\[293\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-295) Argentina subsequently announced, on 5 February, that it would withdraw from WHO, citing "deep differences" with the organization.[\[294\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-296) On 27 May 2025, during a meeting with [US Secretary of Health and Human Services](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/US_Secretary_of_Health_and_Human_Services "US Secretary of Health and Human Services") [Robert F. Kennedy Jr.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_F._Kennedy_Jr. "Robert F. Kennedy Jr.") in Buenos Aires, Milei ratified Argentina's withdrawal from WHO, accusing the organization of basing its positions on political interests and bureaucratic structures while refusing to review what he claimed were mistakes.[\[295\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-297) Argentina finalized its exit from WHO on 17 March 2026.[\[296\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-298)

The [relations between Argentina and Spain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Argentina%E2%80%93Spain_relations "Argentina–Spain relations") drastically deteriorated under President Milei, a staunch opponent of Spanish Prime Minister [Pedro Sánchez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pedro_S%C3%A1nchez "Pedro Sánchez").[\[297\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-299)[\[298\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-300) Milei strongly rejects the [PSOE](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PSOE "PSOE") and has called Spain a [socialist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Socialist "Socialist") as well as a [failed country](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Failed_country "Failed country").[\[299\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-301)[\[300\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-302) In August 2026, Milei stated that "the end of socialism gets closer," making a reference to [the migrant crisis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Morocco%E2%80%93Spain_border_incident "2026 Morocco–Spain border incident") in [Ceuta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ceuta "Ceuta") as a result of the crossing of over 60,000 illegal immigrants into the Spanish territory.[\[301\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-303)[\[302\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-304)

In October 2025, Milei and the government of Argentina signed the nomination of Donald Trump for the [2025 Nobel Peace Prize](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Nobel_Peace_Prize "2025 Nobel Peace Prize"), which was later won by [Venezuelan opposition](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Venezuelan_opposition "Venezuelan opposition") leader [María Corina Machado](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mar%C3%ADa_Corina_Machado "María Corina Machado").[\[303\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-305) Milei congratulated her for "fighting against the narcodictatorship in Venezuela".[\[304\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-306)

[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/be/Leaders_on_the_Shield_of_the_Americas_summit_2026.jpg/250px-Leaders_on_the_Shield_of_the_Americas_summit_2026.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Leaders_on_the_Shield_of_the_Americas_summit_2026.jpg) Milei at the [Shield of the Americas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shield_of_the_Americas "Shield of the Americas") summit in March 2026

That same month, Milei ordered to vote in favor of the [US embargo on Cuba](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_embargo_against_Cuba "United States embargo against Cuba"). It was the first time in history that Argentina supported the blockade, voting along six other countries: the US, Israel, Hungary, [Paraguay](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paraguay "Paraguay"), [North Macedonia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/North_Macedonia "North Macedonia"), and Ukraine.[\[305\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-307)[\[306\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-308) In January 2026, Milei celebrated the [capture of Nicolás Maduro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_United_States_strikes_in_Venezuela "2026 United States strikes in Venezuela") on social media, posting on [X](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Twitter "Twitter"): "liberty advances, _[¡Viva la libertad, carajo!](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/%C2%A1Viva_la_libertad,_carajo! "¡Viva la libertad, carajo!")_".[\[307\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-309) In a later interview, Milei expressed support for the US taking charge of the oil industry in Venezuela, which Milei said will "cut supply to communists."[\[285\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-china2026-287)

Milei's approach to Israel includes moving the Argentine embassy from [Tel Aviv](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tel_Aviv "Tel Aviv") to [Jerusalem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jerusalem "Jerusalem"); he made a state visit to Israel on 6 February 2024, in which he announced plans to [relocate the embassy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Status_of_Jerusalem "Status of Jerusalem").[\[308\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-310) On 11 June 2025, while addressing the [Knesset](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Knesset "Knesset"), Milei confirmed that Argentina would effectively move its embassy to Jerusalem.[\[309\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-311) Milei met with Israeli FM [Gideon Sa'ar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gideon_Sa'ar "Gideon Sa'ar") in November 2025, announcing that he planned a trip to Israel between April and May 2026 to officially inaugurate the Argentine embassy in Jerusalem, with FM [Pablo Quirno](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pablo_Quirno "Pablo Quirno") making a previous trip to formalize the move in February.[\[310\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-312)[\[311\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-313) In January 2026, it was reported that the government had paused its plans to relocate the embassy pending the resolution of Israeli business in the Falkland Islands.[\[312\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-mendozaonlineembassy-314)[\[313\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-315) However, Milei reactivated the move of the embassy in April 2026, intending the officialize the decision on a visit to Israel that month.[\[314\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-argclarin-316)[\[315\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-pag12arg-317)[\[316\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-galliganiinfobae-318)[\[317\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-319)

In January 2026, Milei signed a decree officially designating the [Quds Force](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quds_Force "Quds Force") of [Iran](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iran "Iran")'s [Islamic Revolutionary Guard Corps](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islamic_Revolutionary_Guard_Corps "Islamic Revolutionary Guard Corps") (IRGC) as a terrorist organization over the [crackdown](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Iran_massacres "2026 Iran massacres") on [Iranian protests](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025%E2%80%932026_Iranian_protests "2025–2026 Iranian protests").[\[318\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-320) Following the outbreak of the [Iran war](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Iran_war "2026 Iran war") with U.S.–Israeli strikes on Iran in February 2026, Milei celebrated the [assassination of Ali Khamenei](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_Ali_Khamenei "Assassination of Ali Khamenei"), accusing [Khamenei](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ali_Khamenei "Ali Khamenei") and the Islamic government of [sponsoring terrorist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iran_and_state-sponsored_terrorism "Iran and state-sponsored terrorism") acts like the [AMIA bombing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AMIA_bombing "AMIA bombing").[\[319\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-321) Milei has stated that Iran is an enemy of Argentina, citing past terrorist attacks on Argentine soil allegedly supported by the Iranian regime.[\[320\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-322) He has also supported attacks on Iran and since February 2026, his government [adopted a policy of total support](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Argentina_in_the_2026_Iran_war "Argentina in the 2026 Iran war") for Israel and the United States in the Iran war, promoting Israel as a defender of [Judeo-Christian values](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Judeo-Christian_ethics "Judeo-Christian ethics") and [Western civilization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Western_civilization "Western civilization").[\[321\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-323)[\[322\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-mileisinterviewjcv-324) In March 2026, Milei referred to himself as the "most [Zionist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zionist "Zionist") president in the world."[\[323\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-jpostzionism-325)[\[324\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-326) In April 2026, Milei made his [third state visit to Israel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_state_visit_by_Javier_Milei_to_Israel "2026 state visit by Javier Milei to Israel"). He was honored as one of the 14 torchbearers in the national Israeli [Independence Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Independence_Day_(Israel) "Independence Day (Israel)") [ceremony](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Torch-lighting_Ceremony_(Israel) "Torch-lighting Ceremony (Israel)").[\[325\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-327)

Milei's support for Israel made him the first [non-Jew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Non-Jew "Non-Jew") to receive the [Genesis Prize](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Genesis_Prize "Genesis Prize"), dubbed the "Jewish Nobel" in 2025. Milei's $1 million award supported the idea of creating the [Isaac Accords](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Isaac_Accords "Isaac Accords") to deepen ties between Israel and [Latin America](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_America "Latin America").[\[326\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-328)

### Cryptocurrency scandal

Main article: [$Libra cryptocurrency scandal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/$Libra_cryptocurrency_scandal "$Libra cryptocurrency scandal")

In February 2025, Milei publicly promoted the [cryptocurrency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cryptocurrency "Cryptocurrency") $LIBRA, a [meme coin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Meme_coin "Meme coin"), on his X account; the coin suffered a disastrous price drop shortly afterwards. Commentators have said that it could have been a [rug pull](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Exit_scam "Exit scam").[\[327\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-329)[\[328\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-330) The resulting [controversy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/$Libra_cryptocurrency_scandal "$Libra cryptocurrency scandal") was dubbed [Cryptogate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_-gate_scandals_and_controversies#Cryptogate "List of -gate scandals and controversies") by the media.[\[329\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-331)[\[330\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-332)[\[331\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-333) _[The Economist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Economist "The Economist")_ called it the "first big scandal" of Milei's presidency.[\[332\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-334)

In response, opposition politicians in [Congress](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Argentine_National_Congress "Argentine National Congress") called for Milei's impeachment, and a group led by [Claudio Lozano](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Claudio_Lozano?action=edit&redlink=1 "Claudio Lozano (page does not exist)")\[ [es](https://es.wikipedia.org/wiki/Claudio%20Lozano "es:Claudio Lozano")\], the leader of the opposition [Popular Unity](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Popular_Unity_(Argentina) "Popular Unity (Argentina)") party, filed charges of fraud against Milei.[\[333\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-335)[\[334\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-336)[\[335\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-337) However, the initiative does not have enough supporters in the Impeachment Commission to proceed with the proposal.[\[336\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-338) Milei conceded that promoting the coin was a mistake on his part, noting that he did not participate in its development.[\[337\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-:5-339) However, reports suggest close associates of Milei, Mauricio Novelli and Manuel Terrones Godoy, were in touch with LIBRA's creator Hayden Davis, who was responsible for the launch of the token.[\[338\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-340) Novelli was reported to have accepted bribes in exchange for providing access to the president and having facilitated the president's promotion of the token; although there is no evidence that Milei was aware of this.[\[339\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-341) Milei and his government have defended him and said that he regularly promotes private Argentine ventures and projects online on his X account to attract investments and create jobs; and will continue to do so. One government official described Milei as the scam's biggest victim and said that he was cheated.[\[340\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-342)[\[341\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-343)[\[342\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-:4-344) Because of the rug pull, Milei ordered the Anti-Corruption office to determine whether any improper conduct occurred by any member of the national government, including himself. However, this office is part of the executive branch, and its director was placed in office by Milei.[\[343\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-345) Milei's lawyer stated that Milei's behaviour "does not constitute any crime, due to the lack of intention".[\[337\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-:5-339) Milei also stated in an interview that he would seek legal advice from [Mariano Cúneo Libarona](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mariano_C%C3%BAneo_Libarona "Mariano Cúneo Libarona"), the [minister of justice](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Justice_and_Human_Rights_(Argentina) "Ministry of Justice and Human Rights (Argentina)"), an action seen as an acknowledgement that the promotion was an official action as president.[\[344\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-346) However, he also argued that the "volatility traders" who lost funds understood the risks involved.[\[342\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-:4-344)

### Job approval

Javier Milei's approval rating has been volatile throughout his presidency, coinciding with scandals, the state of the economy, improvement and deterioration and elections. Milei's victory in the elections of 2023 were not due to widespread approval of his ideas or policies, but moreso as a form of protest against the establishment parties.

In a [Gallup poll](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gallup_poll "Gallup poll") on 10 December 2024, the approval ratings for Milei were around 50%, with trust in the government having doubled since 2023. The perception of the economic situation and the standard of living has also improved, with 53% of Argentines believing that their standard of living is now better, reaching the same high as 2015. 41% believe the economy of their city is also improving, reflecting a more optimistic view of Argentina. However, 69% of Argentines believe that it is a bad time to look for a job in this economy, while 35% do not have enough money for food, although these figures are a modest decrease from their two-decade high in 2019. 59% of the richest 20%, but only 39% of the poorest 20% approve of Milei's governance, though the ratings are still higher across the socioeconomic board when compared to Milei's predecessor, [Alberto Fernández](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alberto_Fern%C3%A1ndez "Alberto Fernández").[\[190\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Vigers-192)

Through 2024 in to March 2025, Milei's [approval rating](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Approval_rating "Approval rating") declined from 49% to 47% with his disapproval numbers rising from 48% to 49%. His two predecessors, [Alberto Fernández](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alberto_Fern%C3%A1ndez "Alberto Fernández") and [Mauricio Macri](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mauricio_Macri "Mauricio Macri") saw a 50% to 28% and 72% to 45% drop during the same periods in office. The issues cited most commonly by respondents were the current IMF negotiations and the handling of the Bahia Blanca natural disaster.[\[345\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-347)[\[346\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-348)

The corruption scandal revealed in August 2025 had a profound impact on Milei's approval rating, shifting from a steady 48% just a month before to 39%. In late August, local outlets released audio recordings that allegedly captured a high-ranking government official talking about bribes and implying that Karina Milei, the president's sister and chief of staff, was receiving kickback payments. Javier Milei has rejected the claims as false, while Karina has not publicly addressed them. His supporters have claimed that his opponents, the Peronists and Kirchnerists have framed or otherwise.[\[347\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-349) Despite the scandal Milei's party, La Libertad Avanza remains as one of the most popular parties in Argentina.[\[348\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-350)

On 25 September 2025, the approval rating for Javier Milei was at an all-time low of 32.1%, largely due to the Jose Espert's — La Libertad Avanza's lead candidate for the Chamber of Deputies in Buenos Aires Province — ties to drug traffickers being exposed as well as the economic crisis of September 2025.[\[349\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-351)[\[350\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-352) Despite disapproval ratings soaring, Milei's party attained a larger than expected victory in the legislative elections of 2025.[\[351\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-353) According to political scientist Carlos Fara, the rejection of Peronism “carried more weight” than the recent political and corruption scandals surrounding Milei's government, the run on the peso and “the fatigue with the President’s leadership style." This was reflected in the low turnout of 67 percent, the lowest figure since 1983 in a country with compulsory voting.[\[352\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-354)

By late November 2025, his approval rating jumped between 42.6% and 49%. Men have a significantly more positive image of Milei than women.[\[353\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-355)[\[354\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-356) As of late May 2026, Milei's approval rating increased to 40%, while disapproval ratings fell from 63% compared to the previous months to 58%.[\[355\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-357)

According to a poll by AtlasIntel, conducted for Bloomberg News, Milei's approval ratings have dropped to 37.1% by August 2026. As the years go by there is an increasing divergence, where most people who were undecided on the matter have picked a side. There is a trend of increasing skepticism and opposition towards the president, initially occurring in June 2025 but temporarily cushioned after his victory in the midterm elections of 2025. 62.4% of the Argentinian population disapproves of Milei's presidency.[\[356\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-358)

## Political positions

Main article: [Political positions of Javier Milei](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Political_positions_of_Javier_Milei "Political positions of Javier Milei")

Milei advocates [minimal government](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Night-watchman_state "Night-watchman state"), focusing on administering justice and ensuring security,[\[4\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Il_Post_2023-6) with a philosophy rooted in [life, liberty, and property](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Life,_Liberty_and_the_pursuit_of_Happiness#Lockean_roots_hypothesis "Life, Liberty and the pursuit of Happiness"), and [free market](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_market "Free market") principles. He criticizes [socialism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Socialism "Socialism") and [communism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Communism "Communism"),[\[357\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Cronista_2019-359) advocating [economic liberalization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Economic_liberalization "Economic liberalization") and restructuring of government ministries.[\[358\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-360) He opposes Argentina's Central Bank and current taxation policies.[\[359\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Centenera_&_Criales_2023-361)[\[360\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-362)

Economically, Milei is influenced by the [Austrian school](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Austrian_school_of_economics "Austrian school of economics"), and admires former President [Carlos Menem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carlos_Menem "Carlos Menem")'s policies.[\[361\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Fiore_Viani_2021-363) He supports [capitalism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Capitalism "Capitalism"), viewing socialism as embodying envy and coercion.[\[357\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Cronista_2019-359) Milei proposes reducing government ministries and addressing economic challenges through spending cuts and fiscal reforms, criticizing previous administrations for excessive spending.[\[7\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Viriglio_2023-9)[\[362\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-364) He has praised the monetary policies of former British prime minister [Margaret Thatcher](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Margaret_Thatcher "Margaret Thatcher"), and called her "a great leader".[\[363\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-365)[\[364\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-366)[\[365\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-367)

Milei opposes [abortion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abortion "Abortion")[\[366\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-368) and [euthanasia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Euthanasia "Euthanasia"),[\[367\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-369) and supports [privatization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Privatization "Privatization") in education and health care. He opposes [mandatory vaccination](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vaccination_policy "Vaccination policy"), and supports [drug legalization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Drug_liberalization "Drug liberalization") and the [legalization of prostitution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prostitution_law#Regulated_prostitution "Prostitution law").[\[368\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Rossi_2021-370)[\[369\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-El_Cronista_2023-371) As a supporter of the [right to keep and bear arms](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Right_to_keep_and_bear_arms "Right to keep and bear arms"), Milei advocates for the deregulation of firearm ownership, and proposes [immigration restrictions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opposition_to_immigration "Opposition to immigration") for criminals.[\[4\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Il_Post_2023-6)[\[370\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-372) In foreign policy, Milei criticizes the [IMF](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_Monetary_Fund "International Monetary Fund"),[\[371\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Nugent_2023-373) opposes [trade unions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trade_union "Trade union"),[\[372\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Infonews.com_2023-374) aligns with [anti-socialist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Criticism_of_socialism "Criticism of socialism") figures of the [Americas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Americas "Americas") like [Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Donald_Trump "Donald Trump") and [Jair Bolsonaro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jair_Bolsonaro "Jair Bolsonaro"),[\[373\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-375)[\[374\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Gosman_2023-376) and prioritizes alliances with the United States and Israel.[\[375\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-377)[\[376\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-378) Milei has disputed the estimated death toll of the Dirty War, and removed funding for special prosecutors, claiming it violates the country's constitution.[\[377\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-379) He had been critical of relations with China because of its communist ideology during his presidential campaign, but he came to see China as a business partner in the first year of his presidency.[\[378\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-380)[\[379\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-381) He supports Ukraine against Russia in the latter's [ongoing invasion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_invasion_of_Ukraine "Russian invasion of Ukraine").[\[380\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-382) He opposes the [transgender rights movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Transgender_rights_movement "Transgender rights movement"), and what he calls a "cult of [gender ideology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-gender_movement "Anti-gender movement")". He repealed the decree that established a job quota for transgender people in the public sector.[\[381\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-383)[\[382\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-384)[\[383\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-385)

Milei advocates for dialogue with the [United Kingdom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Kingdom "United Kingdom") over the [Falkland Islands sovereignty dispute](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Falkland_Islands_sovereignty_dispute "Falkland Islands sovereignty dispute").[\[56\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Alonso_2023-58) In May 2024, he indicated, for the time being, his general acceptance of the [Falkland Islands](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Falkland_Islands "Falkland Islands")' status as a [British Overseas Territory](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/British_Overseas_Territories "British Overseas Territories"), accepting that it could take decades for Argentina to regain control of them. Though asserting that Argentina would not "relinquish \[our\] sovereignty" over the islands, Milei said he would not "seek conflict with the United Kingdom", preferring to resolve the dispute "within the framework of peace".[\[384\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-decades_2024_05_06_bbc-386)

Javier Milei has adopted a strongly pro-Israel foreign policy stance since taking office. According to Reuters, his administration has expressed intentions to strengthen diplomatic relations with Israel, and re-orient Argentina's foreign policy closer to Israeli positions. Milei has considered relocating Argentina's embassy from Tel Aviv to Jerusalem, a move that would align Argentina with a small number of countries recognizing Jerusalem as Israel's capital. He is one of the most pro-Israel leaders in Latin America, particularly following his election and subsequent diplomatic engagements with Israeli officials.[\[385\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-387)[\[386\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-388)[\[387\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-389)

## Public image

Main article: [Public image of Javier Milei](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Public_image_of_Javier_Milei "Public image of Javier Milei")

Milei has cultivated a complex and controversial public image marked by a blend of [populist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Populism "Populism"), right-wing libertarian, and [conservative](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conservatism "Conservatism") ideologies. Known for his [ultra-liberal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ultraliberalism#Market_liberalism "Ultraliberalism") economic views and [right-wing populist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Right-wing_populism "Right-wing populism") rhetoric, Milei's political stance has been subject to various interpretations by international media and political commentators.[\[55\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Molina_2023-57)[\[56\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Alonso_2023-58) His rise to prominence during the 2023 presidential campaign, fueled by his primary win, sparked widespread attention,[\[101\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Reuters_2023-103)[\[102\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Al_Jazeera_2023-104)[\[103\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Go%C3%B1i_2023-105) as did his [central bank](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Central_bank "Central bank") abolition and [dollarization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Currency_substitution "Currency substitution") proposals.[\[388\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Sivak_2023-390)

[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/38/Javier_Milei_recibe_a_Viktor_Orb%C3%A1n_2.jpg/250px-Javier_Milei_recibe_a_Viktor_Orb%C3%A1n_2.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Javier_Milei_recibe_a_Viktor_Orb%C3%A1n_2.jpg) Milei with Hungarian Prime Minister [Viktor Orbán](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Viktor_Orb%C3%A1n "Viktor Orbán")

Milei is known for his flamboyant personality, distinctive personal style, and strong media presence, which sometimes causes controversy, and his embrace of [conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conspiracy_theory "Conspiracy theory"),[\[389\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-391) including the [Cultural Marxism conspiracy theory](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cultural_Marxism_conspiracy_theory "Cultural Marxism conspiracy theory").[\[390\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-392) He has also called the idea of [climate change](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Climate_change "Climate change") as an anthropogenic phenomenon "a socialist lie" and said that concerns about it are nothing more than "deceptions promoted by the neo-Marxists",[\[391\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-393)[\[374\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Gosman_2023-376) as are those related to the [attempts to overturn the 2020 United States presidential election](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Attempts_to_overturn_the_2020_United_States_presidential_election "Attempts to overturn the 2020 United States presidential election").[\[392\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-394) Echoing [Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Donald_Trump "Donald Trump"), he also claimed electoral fraud ahead of the 2023 presidential runoff.[\[393\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-395)[\[394\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-396) Like other right-wing populists, his rhetoric focuses on opposing what he calls "the political caste".[\[395\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Dadouch_2023-397) Milei's party was criticized for including among his candidates apologists for the [National Reorganization Process](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Reorganization_Process "National Reorganization Process").[\[75\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-iProfesional_2023-77) During his political career, Milei has also been involved in several investigations and has been accused of having a violent attitude toward journalists and critics, as well as of misogynistic behavior, including toward women in journalism.[\[40\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-BBC_News_Mundo_2023-42)

Milei is a [cosplayer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cosplay "Cosplay") with a [superhero](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Superhero "Superhero") persona called "General AnCap".[\[396\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-apnews-398) He also champions [free love](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_love "Free love").[\[397\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-399)[\[398\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-400)[\[399\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-401) In addition to being nicknamed _el Peluca_ ("The Wig") for his eccentric hair,[\[400\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-402)[\[401\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-403)[\[402\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-404) which has been compared to that of Trump and fellow right-wing populists [Boris Johnson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boris_Johnson "Boris Johnson") and [Geert Wilders](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Geert_Wilders "Geert Wilders"),[\[403\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-405) Milei is known as _el Loco_ ("The Madman").[\[404\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Gonz%C3%A1lez_2023-406) News outlets have likened him to both Trump and [Wolverine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wolverine_(character) "Wolverine (character)").[\[405\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-407) His admirers also call him "The Lion" because of the lyrics of the song "Panic Show" by rock band [La Renga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_Renga "La Renga"), which he sings in his public acts, coupled with his looks (in particular, his long hair).[\[396\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-apnews-398)[\[406\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-408) _[Time](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_(magazine) "Time (magazine)")_ named him one of the [100 most influential people in the world](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_100 "Time 100") in 2024 and 2025.[\[407\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-auto3-409)[\[408\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-auto1-410)

[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/95/Elon_Musk_%26_Javier_Milei_%2854348704622%29.jpg/250px-Elon_Musk_%26_Javier_Milei_%2854348704622%29.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Elon_Musk_&_Javier_Milei_(54348704622).jpg) Milei with [Elon Musk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elon_Musk "Elon Musk") holding a chainsaw; the chainsaw has become a popular symbol associated with Milei.

The [chainsaw](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chainsaw "Chainsaw") has become an enduring and popular symbol associated with Milei[\[409\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-411) (he has been called the "chainsaw candidate"[\[410\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-412)), specifically symbolizing his "cutting" of regulations, bureaucracy, and [red tape](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_tape "Red tape") in Argentina. Some commentators have called Milei's economic policy of cutting regulations "chainsaw economics".[\[411\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-413) Milei's supporters often carry chainsaws at rallies, symbolizing "his promise to cut down the size of the state".[\[412\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-414) Milei is also well known for his phrase " [¡Viva la libertad, carajo!](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/%C2%A1Viva_la_libertad,_carajo! "¡Viva la libertad, carajo!")", which is uttered most especially at the end of various speeches given by him.[\[413\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-415)

### Other

Milei was awarded the [2025 Genesis Prize](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Genesis_Prize "2025 Genesis Prize") in recognition of his support of Israel.[\[418\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-420) He was also awarded an honorary doctorate from [Bar-Ilan University](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bar-Ilan_University "Bar-Ilan University") in 2026.[\[419\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-421) _[Time](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_(magazine) "Time (magazine)")_ named him one of the [100 most influential people in the world](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_100 "Time 100") in 2024 and 2025.[\[407\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-auto3-409)[\[408\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-auto1-410)

## Personal life

[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/ad/Milei_inicia_en_Nueva_York_su_primera_visita_a_EEUU_como_presidente_electo_de_Argentina.png/250px-Milei_inicia_en_Nueva_York_su_primera_visita_a_EEUU_como_presidente_electo_de_Argentina.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Milei_inicia_en_Nueva_York_su_primera_visita_a_EEUU_como_presidente_electo_de_Argentina.png) Milei (center) praying at [the grave](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ohel_(Chabad-Lubavitch) "Ohel (Chabad-Lubavitch)") of [Menachem Mendel Schneerson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Menachem_Mendel_Schneerson "Menachem Mendel Schneerson") on 27 November 2023

Milei is unmarried, and, while he was a presidential candidate, said that, if elected, he would have [his sister](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karina_Milei "Karina Milei") take the role of [First Lady of Argentina](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/First_Lady_of_Argentina "First Lady of Argentina").[\[395\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Dadouch_2023-397) However, in December 2023, after winning the presidency, he announced that his government would not have a first lady.[\[420\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-:3-422) He called the role "anachronistic", and declared that this decision was taken after discussions with both his sister and his girlfriend, actress [Fátima Flórez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/F%C3%A1tima_Fl%C3%B3rez "Fátima Flórez").[\[420\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-:3-422) Milei originally announced he was dating Fátima Florez in August 2023.[\[421\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-423) Previously, he dated singer [Daniela Mori](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniela_Mori?action=edit&redlink=1 "Daniela Mori (page does not exist)")\[ [es](https://es.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniela%20Mori "es:Daniela Mori")\].[\[422\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-424)

While raised [Catholic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholic "Catholic"),[\[423\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-425)[\[424\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-426)[\[425\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Gillespie_&_Tobias_2023-427) Milei had been critical of the [Catholic Church](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholic_Church "Catholic Church") under [Pope Francis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_Francis "Pope Francis"),[\[426\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-428)[\[427\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-429) and his disparaging comments about Francis attracted criticism from Catholics.[\[428\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-430)[\[429\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-431) Conversely, Milei would later embrace Francis after meeting him, stating in a [Retequattro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rete_4 "Rete 4") interview, "I had to reconsider some positions, and starting from that moment, we began to build a positive relationship."[\[430\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-432)[\[431\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-433) Milei also reads the [Torah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Torah "Torah") daily, and has visited [the grave](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ohel_(Chabad-Lubavitch) "Ohel (Chabad-Lubavitch)") of Orthodox rabbi [Menachem Mendel Schneerson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Menachem_Mendel_Schneerson "Menachem Mendel Schneerson").[\[432\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-434)[\[433\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-435)[\[434\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-436) Before November 2023, Milei said he had contemplated [converting to Judaism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conversion_to_Judaism "Conversion to Judaism"), but that observing the [Jewish Sabbath](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_Sabbath "Jewish Sabbath") could pose challenges if he became president.[\[388\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Sivak_2023-390)[\[435\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-437)[\[436\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-438) Upon being elected president, it was reported that Milei intended to convert to Judaism.[\[425\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Gillespie_&_Tobias_2023-427) Milei has confessed that he professes a "fanaticism" for [Judaism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Judaism "Judaism").[\[16\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-:2-18)

Milei owns five cloned [English Mastiffs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/English_Mastiff "English Mastiff"), their progenitor being [Conan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conan_(Javier_Milei's_dog) "Conan (Javier Milei's dog)"), who died in 2017 after suffering from [spinal cancer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spinal_cancer "Spinal cancer").[\[75\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-iProfesional_2023-77)[\[404\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Gonz%C3%A1lez_2023-406)[\[437\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-El_Doce_2023-439) He considers Conan his son (a form of [pet humanization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pet_humanization "Pet humanization")), and has named four of Conan's six [clones](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cloning "Cloning"), including one named after the original and another named Angelito,[\[438\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-440) Milton (in honor of [Milton Friedman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Milton_Friedman "Milton Friedman")), Murray (in honor of [Murray Rothbard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Murray_Rothbard "Murray Rothbard")), Robert, and Lucas (both named after [Robert Lucas, Jr.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_Lucas_Jr. "Robert Lucas Jr.")).[\[439\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-441)[\[440\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-442) Milei said that he cloned Conan because he understands cloning as "a way of approaching eternity".[\[437\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-El_Doce_2023-439) To do this, he went to a clinic in the United States. The process cost him about $50,000.[\[437\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-El_Doce_2023-439) He has called his dogs his "four-legged children", and thanked them after his electoral win.[\[7\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-Viriglio_2023-9)[\[441\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-443) Milei commented that one day, there was a fire at his building, while he was watching TV, which he realized because Conan made for the balcony. He escaped with him, using the emergency ladder, and took him to the vet, who gave oxygen to Milei because he was developing symptoms of [cyanosis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cyanosis "Cyanosis").[\[442\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-444)

Milei is an avid fan of [association football](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Association_football "Association football"). Albeit having played for [Chacarita Juniors](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chacarita_Juniors "Chacarita Juniors") in his early adulthood, he has stated to be a supporter of [Boca Juniors](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boca_Juniors "Boca Juniors"); in a 2024 interview with [Radio Mitre](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Radio_Mitre "Radio Mitre"), Milei said, "I am a member and fan of Boca. If investment groups come and put a fortune for us to win all the time, where do I sign?"[\[443\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-445) However, he has expressed criticism of the presidency of [Juan Román Riquelme](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Juan_Rom%C3%A1n_Riquelme "Juan Román Riquelme") at the club since 2023, stating that his "management at Boca is a disaster", and referred to Riquelme as "a [Kirchnerist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kirchnerism "Kirchnerism") running the club".[\[444\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-446) Milei is a huge fan of the English rock band [The Rolling Stones](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Rolling_Stones "The Rolling Stones").[\[445\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-447)

In December 2024, while holding the office of Head of State, Milei applied for, and was granted, [Italian citizenship](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_nationality_law "Italian nationality law") via _[jus sanguinis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jus_sanguinis "Jus sanguinis")_, under the government of Prime Minister [Giorgia Meloni](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Giorgia_Meloni "Giorgia Meloni").[\[446\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-448)[\[447\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Milei#cite_note-449)
